Every command that\nwrites transactions (load, post, amend, delete, transfer) calls this one check before its first\nwrite. An account's balance is the sum of its stored transaction amounts in integer cents.\n\n## Interface\n\nModule `balance_rules`, importable from the application root.\n\n`check_final_balances(db, inserts=(), amount_changes=(), deletions=())` returns `None` or raises\n`ledger_errors.Rejected`.\n\n| Argument | Meaning |\n|----------|---------|\n| `db` | An open persistence `Database` (from `open_database` or `db_session`). |\n| `inserts` | Iterable of `(account, amount_cents)` pending new rows. |\n| `amount_changes` | Iterable of `(txn_id, new_amount_cents)` replacing a stored row's amount. |\n| `deletions` | Iterable of `txn_id` of stored rows to be removed. |\n\n## Rules\n\n- Read stored rows only through `db.transactions.list(account=...)` and `db.transactions.get(txn_id=...)`.\n- For each account touched by any pending change, compute `stored sum + pending inserts + amount deltas - deleted amounts`, in Python `int` cents. No floating point.\n- Judge only the final state after all pending changes. An intermediate dip that a later pending change restores is allowed, whatever the order of the arguments.\n- If any touched account's final balance is below zero, raise `Rejected`. A balance of exactly zero is allowed.\n- The check performs no write of any kind.\n- A `txn_id` in `amount_changes` or `deletions` that is not stored is ignored here; the calling command reports it as not found before calling this check.\n- The message of `Rejected` never contains a DSN or credential.\n\n## Database Interface\n\n- `FEATURE-TXN-003` — read: list a transaction set by account - supplies: `account` - returns: `txn_id`, `date`, `account`, `category`, `amount`, `memo`\n- `FEATURE-TXN-002` — read: get a transaction by id - supplies: `txn_id` - returns: `txn_id`, `date`, `account`, `category`, `amount`, `memo`\n\n## Programmatic Acceptance\n\n=== AC balance-rejects-negative-insert ===\nIntent: A pending insert that leaves an account below zero raises Rejected and writes nothing.\n\nfrom persistence.testing import db_session\nfrom balance_rules import check_final_balances\nfrom ledger_errors import Rejected\n\nacct = \"ZQBR01\"\ncat = \"ZQBRC01\"\nwith db_session() as db:\n    db.accounts.create(account=acct, name=\"Balance One\")\n    db.categories.create(category=cat, description=\"Balance One\")\n    before = len(db.transactions.list())\n    try:\n        check_final_balances(db, inserts=[(acct, -100)])\n    except Rejected:\n        pass\n    else:\n        raise AssertionError(\"expected Rejected\")\n    assert len(db.transactions.list()) == before\n=== END AC balance-rejects-negative-insert ===\n\n=== AC balance-final-state-only ===\nIntent: An intermediate dip restored later in the same command is allowed; zero is allowed.\n\nfrom persistence.testing import db_session\nfrom balance_rules import check_final_balances\n\nacct = \"ZQBR02\"\nwith db_session() as db:\n    db.accounts.create(account=acct, name=\"Balance Two\")\n    check_final_balances(db, inserts=[(acct, -500), (acct, 1000)])\n    check_final_balances(db, inserts=[(acct, 700), (acct, -700)])\n=== END AC balance-final-state-only ===\n\n=== AC balance-amend ===\nIntent: An amount change that drives the stored balance below zero is rejected, a safe one is accepted.\n\nfrom persistence.testing import db_session\nfrom balance_rules import check_final_balances\nfrom ledger_errors import Rejected\n\nacct = \"ZQBR03\"\ncat = \"ZQBRC03\"\ntxn_id = 910002\nwith db_session() as db:\n    db.accounts.create(account=acct, name=\"Balance Three\")\n    db.categories.create(category=cat, description=\"Balance Three\")\n    db.transactions.create(\n        txn_id=txn_id, date=\"2026-04-01\", account=acct, category=cat, amount=1000, memo=\"\"\n    )\n    check_final_balances(db, amount_changes=[(txn_id, 1)])\n    try:\n        check_final_balances(db, amount_changes=[(txn_id, -1)])\n    except Rejected:\n        pass\n    else:\n        raise AssertionError(\"expected Rejected\")\n    assert db.transactions.get(txn_id=txn_id).amount == 1000\n=== END AC balance-amend ===\n\n=== AC balance-delete ===\nIntent: Deleting a credit that funds a debit is rejected; deleting the debit is accepted.\n\nfrom persistence.testing import db_session\nfrom balance_rules import check_final_balances\nfrom ledger_errors import Rejected\n\nacct = \"ZQBR04\"\ncat = \"ZQBRC04\"\ncredit_id = 910003\ndebit_id = 910004\nwith db_session() as db:\n    db.accounts.create(account=acct, name=\"Balance Four\")\n    db.categories.create(category=cat, description=\"Balance Four\")\n    db.transactions.create(\n        txn_id=credit_id, date=\"2026-04-01\", account=acct, category=cat, amount=1000, memo=\"\"\n    )\n    db.transactions.create(\n        txn_id=debit_id, date=\"2026-04-02\", account=acct, category=cat, amount=-400, memo=\"\"\n    )\n    check_final_balances(db, deletions=[debit_id])\n    try:\n        check_final_balances(db, deletions=[credit_id])\n    except Rejected:\n        pass\n    else:\n        raise AssertionError(\"expected Rejected\")\n    assert db.transactions.get(txn_id=credit_id) is not None\n    assert db.transactions.get(txn_id=debit_id) is not None\n=== END AC balance-delete ===\n\n## User Acceptance\n\n- None.\n\n## Guardrails\n\n- Integer cents only; ===\n=== BEGIN ARTIFACT FEATURE-LoadAccounts.md ===\n# FEATURE: Load Accounts\n\n| Field | Value |\n|-------|-------|\n| Version | 20261026 V1 |\n| Description | Implement `ledger load accounts <csv>` as an all-or-nothing bulk load. |\n| Depends On | FEATURE-LedgerInit.md, FEATURE-Validation.md, FEATURE-CsvOutput.md, FEATURE-ErrorContract.md, DATABASE.md |\n| Provides | ledger load accounts |\n| Consumes | ledger.register_command, persistence.open_database, validation.check_code, validation.check_text, csv_output.write_rows, ledger_errors.Rejected, ledger_errors.OperationalFailure |\n\n## Questions\n\n- None.\n\n## Purpose\n\nLoad account records from a CSV file. The whole file is checked before the first write, so a\nrejected file stores nothing.\n\n## Rules\n\n- Register the `accounts` table under `load`; the command takes the table name and one file path.\n- An unreadable file raises `OperationalFailure` (exit 1).\n- Parse the whole file with the standard `csv` module. The header must equal `account,name` exactly, in names and order; any other header raises `Rejected` (exit 3) before any row is stored. An empty file counts as a wrong header.\n- Every data row must have exactly two fields. Validate `account` with `validation.check_code` and `name` with `validation.check_text` (1 to 40 characters, no comma or newline). Any bad row rejects the file.\n- A code that repeats within the file, or that already exists in the store (`db.accounts.get`), rejects the whole file. Account names may repeat across accounts.\n- Only after every check passes, insert all rows with `db.accounts.create` in one database transaction. If an insert fails the transaction rolls back and nothing is stored.\n- A header-only file loads zero rows.\n- On success write `table,loaded` and `accounts,<rows>` through `csv_output.write_rows` and exit 0. A failure writes nothing to standard output.\n\n## Database Interface\n\n- `FEATURE-ACCOUNT-002` — read: check whether an account code already exists - supplies: `account` - returns: `account`, `name`\n- `FEATURE-ACCOUNT-001` — write: create an account - supplies: `account`, `name` - returns: `account`, `name`\n\n## Programmatic Acceptance\n\n=== AC load-accounts-success ===\nIntent: A valid == 0\n    with tempfile.TemporaryDirectory() as tmp:\n        path = os.path.join(tmp, \"accounts.csv\")\n        with open(path, \"w\", newline=\"\") as fh:\n            csv.writer(fh, lineterminator=\"\\n\").writerows([header] + rows)\n        code, out, err = cli_runner(\"load\", \"accounts\", path)\n    print(err)\n    assert code == 0\n    assert list(csv.reader(io.StringIO(out))) == [[\"table\", \"loaded\"], [\"accounts\", str(len(rows))]]\n    stored = [[r.account, r.name] for r in db.accounts.list()]\n    assert stored == sorted(rows)\n=== END AC load-accounts-success ===\n\n=== AC load-accounts-header-only ===\nIntent: A header-only file loads zero rows and reports zero.\n\nimport csv\nimport io\nimport os\nimport tempfile\nfrom BEGIN ARTIFACT FEATURE-BalanceRules.md ===\n# FEATURE: Balance Rules\n\n| Field | Value |\n|-------|-------|\n| Version | 20261026 V1 |\n| Description | Judge every affected account balance on the final state of a command and reject any negative balance before a write. |\n| Depends On | DATABASE.md, FEATURE-ErrorContract.md |\n| Provides | balance_rules.check_final_balances |\n| Consumes | persistence.open_database, ledger_errors.Rejected |\n\n## Questions\n\n- None.\n\n## Purpose\n\nRule 2 of the source says no command leaves an account balance below zero. Every command that\nwrites transactions (load, post, amend, delete, transfer) calls this one check before its first\nwrite. An account's balance is the sum of its stored transaction amounts in integer cents.\n\n## Interface\n\nModule `balance_rules`, importable from the application root.\n\n`check_final_balances(db, inserts=(), amount_changes=(), deletions=())` returns `None` or raises\n`ledger_errors.Rejected`.\n\n| Argument | Meaning |\n|----------|---------|\n| `db` | An open persistence `Database` (from `open_database` or `db_session`). |\n| `inserts` | Iterable of `(account, amount_cents)` pending new rows. |\n| `amount_changes` | Iterable of `(txn_id, new_amount_cents)` replacing a stored row's amount. |\n| `deletions` | Iterable of `txn_id` of stored rows to be removed. |\n\n## Rules\n\n- Read stored rows only through `db.transactions.list(account=...)` and `db.transactions.get(txn_id=...)`.\n- For each account touched by any pending change, compute `stored sum + pending inserts + amount deltas - deleted amounts`, in Python `int` cents. No floating point.\n- Judge only the final state after all pending changes. An intermediate dip that a later pending change restores is allowed, whatever the order of the arguments.\n- If any touched account's final balance is below zero, raise `Rejected`. A balance of exactly zero is allowed.\n- The check performs no write of any kind.\n- A `txn_id` in `amount_changes` or `deletions` that is not stored is ignored here; the calling command reports it as not found before calling this check.\n- The message of `Rejected` never contains a DSN or credential.\n\n## Database Interface\n\n- `FEATURE-TXN-003` — read: list a transaction set by account - supplies: `account` - returns: `txn_id`, `date`, `account`, `category`, `amount`, `memo`\n- `FEATURE-TXN-002` — read: get a transaction by id - supplies: `txn_id` - returns: `txn_id`, `date`, `account`, `category`, `amount`, `memo`\n\n## Programmatic Acceptance\n\n=== AC balance-rejects-negative-insert ===\nIntent: A pending insert that leaves an account below zero raises Rejected and writes nothing.\n\nfrom persistence.testing import db_session\nfrom balance_rules import check_final_balances\nfrom ledger_errors import Rejected\n\nacct = \"ZQBR01\"\ncat = \"ZQBRC01\"\nwith db_session() as db:\n    db.accounts.create(account=acct, name=\"Balance One\")\n    db.categories.create(category=cat, description=\"Balance One\")\n    before = len(db.transactions.list())\n    try:\n        check_final_balances(db, inserts=[(acct, -100)])\n    except Rejected:\n        pass\n    else:\n        raise AssertionError(\"expected Rejected\")\n    assert len(db.transactions.list()) == before\n=== END AC balance-rejects-negative-insert ===\n\n=== AC balance-final-state-only ===\nIntent: An intermediate dip restored later in the same command is allowed; zero is allowed.\n\nfrom persistence.testing import db_session\nfrom balance_rules import check_final_balances\n\nacct = \"ZQBR02\"\nwith db_session() as db:\n    db.accounts.create(account=acct, name=\"Balance Two\")\n    check_final_balances(db, inserts=[(acct, -500), (acct, 1000)])\n    check_final_balances(db, inserts=[(acct, 700), (acct, -700)])\n=== END AC balance-final-state-only ===\n\n=== AC balance-amend ===\nIntent: An amount change that drives the stored balance below zero is rejected, a safe one is accepted.\n\nfrom persistence.testing import db_session\nfrom balance_rules import check_final_balances\nfrom ledger_errors import Rejected\n\nacct = \"ZQBR03\"\ncat = \"ZQBRC03\"\ntxn_id = 910002\nwith db_session() as db:\n    db.accounts.create(account=acct, name=\"Balance Three\")\n    db.categories.create(category=cat, description=\"Balance Three\")\n    db.transactions.create(\n        txn_id=txn_id, date=\"2026-04-01\", account=acct, category=cat, amount=1000, memo=\"\"\n    )\n    check_final_balances(db, amount_changes=[(txn_id, 1)])\n    try:\n        check_final_balances(db, amount_changes=[(txn_id, -1)])\n    except Rejected:\n        pass\n    else:\n        raise AssertionError(\"expected Rejected\")\n    assert db.transactions.get(txn_id=txn_id).amount == 1000\n=== END AC balance-amend ===\n\n=== AC balance-delete ===\nIntent: Deleting a credit that funds a debit is rejected; deleting the debit is accepted.\n\nfrom persistence.testing import db_session\nfrom balance_rules import check_final_balances\nfrom ledger_errors import Rejected\n\nacct = \"ZQBR04\"\ncat = \"ZQBRC04\"\ncredit_id = 910003\ndebit_id = 910004\nwith db_session() as db:\n    db.accounts.create(account=acct, name=\"Balance Four\")\n    db.categories.create(category=cat, description=\"Balance Four\")\n    db.transactions.create(\n        txn_id=credit_id, date=\"2026-04-01\", account=acct, category=cat, amount=1000, memo=\"\"\n    )\n    db.transactions.create(\n        txn_id=debit_id, date=\"2026-04-02\", account=acct, category=cat, amount=-400, memo=\"\"\n    )\n    check_final_balances(db, deletions=[debit_id])\n    try:\n        check_final_balances(db, deletions=[credit_id])\n    except Rejected:\n        pass\n    else:\n        raise AssertionError(\"expected Rejected\")\n    assert db.transactions.get(txn_id=credit_id) is not None\n    assert db.transactions.get(txn_id=debit_id) is not None\n=== END AC balance-delete ===\n\n## User Acceptance\n\n- None.\n\n## Guardrails\n\n- Integer cents only; no floating point at any layer.\n- The check never writes to the database.\n- Diagnostics never echo a DSN, path credential or password.\n=== END ARTIFACT ===\n=== BEGIN ARTIFACT FEATURE-CliUsage.md ===\n# FEATURE: CLI Usage\n\n| Field | Value |\n|-------|-------|\n| Version | 20261026 V1 |\n| Description | Reject unknown commands, tables, reports and wrong argument counts with exit 2 and usage text on standard error only. |\n| Depends On | FEATURE-CliFoundation.md, FEATURE-ErrorContract.md |\n| Provides | ledger usage |\n| Consumes | ledger.register_command, ledger_errors.UsageError |\n\n## Questions\n\n- None.\n\n## Purpose\n\nAny invocation that is not a valid command shape exits 2 before any database access. The usage\ntext is derived from the command table and names every command and sub-verb.\n\n## Rules\n\n- No arguments, an unknown command, an unknown `load` table, an unknown `report`, an unknown sub-verb of `account` or `txn`, or the wrong number of arguments for a known command all raise `UsageError`, which the top-level handler maps to exit 2.\n- Argument counts follow the command table: `init` 0, `count` 0, `load` 2, `account add` 2, `account get` 1, `account rename` 2, `account delete` 1, `txn post` 4 or 5, `txn get` 1, `txn amend` 2, `txn delete` 1, `transfer` 4, `report <name>` 0.\n- Usage text goes to standard error only. It lists `init`, `count`, `load`, `account add|get|rename|delete`, `txn post|get|amend|delete`, `transfer`, and `report balances|categories|monthly`.\n- Standard output stays empty and the database is neither opened nor changed.\n- Argument-count validation happens before any value validation, so a command with a wrong count never exits 3.\n\n## Database Interface\n\n- None — this Blueprint reaches no repository.\n\n## Programmatic Acceptance\n\n=== AC usage-no-arguments ===\nIntent: Running with no arguments exits 2 with nothing on standard output.\n\nfrom cli.testing import cli_runner\n\ncode, END AC usage-unknown-report-and-table ===\n\n=== AC usage-wrong-argument-count ===\nIntent: Missing and surplus arguments exit 2 for known commands.\n\nfrom cli.testing import cli_runner\n\ncases = (\"txn\", \"amend\", \"1\"),\n    (\"txn\", \"post\", \"A\", \"B\", \"1.00\"),\n    (\"transfer\", \"A\", \"B\", \"1.00\"),\n    (\"report\", \"balances\", \"extra\"),\n    (\"load\", \"accounts\"),\n]\nfor args in cases:\n    code, out, err = cli_runner(*args)\n    print(args, code, err)\n    assert code == 2, args\n    assert out == \"\", args\n=== END AC usage-wrong-argument-count ===\n\n=== AC usage-changes-nothing ===\nIntent: A usage error leaves every table unchanged.\n\nfrom cli.testing import cli_runner\nfrom persistence.testing import db_session\n\nwith db_session() as db:\n    before = (\n        len(db.accounts.list()),\n        len(db.categories.list()),\n        len(db.transactions.list()),\n    )\n    code, out, err = cli_runner(\"account\", \"add\", \"ONLYCODE\")\n    assert code == 2\n    after = (\n        len(db.accounts.list()),\n        len(db.categories.list()),\n        len(db.transactions.list()),\n    )\n    assert after == before\n=== END AC usage-changes-nothing ===\n\n## User Acceptance\n\n- None.\n\n## Guardrails\n\n- Usage text is written to standard error and never to standard output.\n- A usage error never opens or changes the database.\n=== END ARTIFACT ===\n=== BEGIN ARTIFACT FEATURE-LedgerInit.md ===\n# FEATURE: Ledger Init\n\n| Field | Value |\n|-------|-------|\n| Version | 20261026 V1 |\n| Description | Implement `ledger init` to prepare an empty ledger and remove all rows when rerun. |\n| Depends On | FEATURE-CliFoundation.md, FEATURE-ErrorContract.md, DATABASE.md |\n| Provides | ledger init |\n| Consumes | ledger.register_command, ledger_config.database_dsn, persistence.open_database, ledger_errors.OperationalFailure |\n\n## Questions\n\n- None.\n\n## Purpose\n\n`ledger init` leaves the ledger empty and ready for loading. The tables are created by the\nDATABASE step; `init` only removes rows and never issues DDL.\n\n## Rules\n\n- Register `init` through `ledger.register_command`; it takes no arguments.\n- In one database transaction delete every transaction, then every category, then every account (`db.transactions.delete_all()`, `db.categories.delete_all()`, `db.accounts.delete_all()`), so foreign keys hold at each step.\n- Write nothing to standard output. Exit 0.\n- Rerunning on an empty or populated ledger always succeeds and leaves zero rows in all three tables.\n- The database is resolved by `DATABASE_URL` (an empty value counts as unset), then `DATABASE_PATH` (used only when `DATABASE_URL` is unset), then `data/app.db`.\n- An unreachable database or missing tables raises `OperationalFailure` (exit 1). The diagnostic never contains the DSN, a path credential or a password.\n\n## Database Interface\n\n- `FEATURE-TXN-006` — write: delete every transaction - supplies: none - returns: none\n- `FEATURE-CATEGORY-006` — write: delete every category - supplies: none - returns: none\n- `FEATURE-ACCOUNT-006` — write: delete every account - supplies: none - returns: none\n\n## Programmatic Acceptance\n\n=== AC init-removes-all-rows ===\nIntent: init exits 0, writes nothing to stdout, and removes every row from all three tables.\n\nfrom cli.testing import cli_runner\nfrom persistence.testing import db_session\n\nacct = \"ZQINIT1\"\ncat = \"ZQINITC1\"\nwith db_session() as db:\n    db.accounts.create(account=acct, name=\"Init Account\")\n    db.categories.create(category=cat, description=\"Init Category\")\n    db.transactions.create(\n        txn_id=920001, date=\"2026-04-01\", account=acct, category=cat, amount=100, memo=\"\"\n    )\n    code, out, err = cli_runner(\"init\")\n    print(err)\n    assert code == 0\n    assert out == \"\"\n    assert db.transactions.list() == []\n    assert db.categories.list() == []\n    assert db.accounts.list() == []\n=== END AC init-removes-all-rows ===\n\n=== AC init-rerun ===\nIntent: Rerunning init on an already empty ledger succeeds and stays empty.\n\nfrom cli.testing import cli_runner\nfrom persistence.testing import db_session\n\nwith db_session() as db:\n    for _ in range(2):\n        code, out, err = cli_runner(\"init\")\n        print(err)\n        assert code == 0\n        assert out == \"\"\n    assert db.accounts.list() == []\n    assert db.categories.list() == []\n    assert db.transactions.list() == []\n=== END AC init-rerun ===\n\n=== AC init-wrong-argument-count ===\nIntent: init with an extra argument is a usage error that removes nothing.\n\nfrom cli.testing import cli_runner\nfrom persistence.testing import db_session\n\nacct = \"ZQINIT2\"\nwith db_session() as db:\n    db.accounts.create(account=acct, name=\"Init Keep\")\n    code, out, err = cli_runner(\"init\", \"extra\")\n    assert code == 2\n    assert db.accounts.get(account=acct) is not None\n=== END AC init-wrong-argument-count ===\n\n## User Acceptance\n\n- None.\n\n## Guardrails\n\n- `init` never issues DDL and never drops tables.\n- A failure never writes the database URL, path credential or DSN password to standard output or standard error.\n=== END ARTIFACT ===\n=== BEGIN ARTIFACT FEATURE-LedgerCount.md ===\n# FEATURE: Ledger Count\n\n| ===\n=== BEGIN ARTIFACT FEATURE-LoadAccounts.md ===\n# FEATURE: Load Accounts\n\n| Field | Value |\n|-------|-------|\n| Version | 20261026 V1 |\n| Description | Implement `ledger load accounts <csv>` as an all-or-nothing bulk load. |\n| Depends On | FEATURE-LedgerInit.md, FEATURE-Validation.md, FEATURE-CsvOutput.md, FEATURE-ErrorContract.md, DATABASE.md |\n| Provides | ledger load accounts |\n| Consumes | ledger.register_command, persistence.open_database, validation.check_code, validation.check_text, csv_output.write_rows, ledger_errors.Rejected, ledger_errors.OperationalFailure |\n\n## Questions\n\n- None.\n\n## Purpose\n\nLoad account records from a CSV file. The whole file is checked before the first write, so a\nrejected file stores nothing.\n\n## Rules\n\n- Register the `accounts` table under `load`; the command takes the table name and one file path.\n- An unreadable file raises `OperationalFailure` (exit 1).\n- Parse the whole file with the standard `csv` module. The header must equal `account,name` exactly, in names and order; any other header raises `Rejected` (exit 3) before any row is stored. An empty file counts as a wrong header.\n- Every data row must have exactly two fields. Validate `account` with `validation.check_code` and `name` with `validation.check_text` (1 to 40 characters, no comma or newline). Any bad row rejects the file.\n- A code that repeats within the file, or that already exists in the store (`db.accounts.get`), rejects the whole file. Account names may repeat across accounts.\n- Only after every check passes, insert all rows with `db.accounts.create` in one database transaction. If an insert fails the transaction rolls back and nothing is stored.\n- A header-only file loads zero rows.\n- On success write `table,loaded` and `accounts,<rows>` through `csv_output.write_rows` and exit 0. A failure writes nothing to standard output.\n\n## Database Interface\n\n- `FEATURE-ACCOUNT-002` — read: check whether an account code already exists - supplies: `account` - returns: `account`, `name`\n- `FEATURE-ACCOUNT-001` — write: create an account - supplies: `account`, `name` - returns: `account`, `name`\n\n## Programmatic Acceptance\n\n=== AC load-accounts-success ===\nIntent: A valid file stores every row and reports the loaded count.\n\nimport csv\nimport io\nimport os\nimport tempfile\nfrom cli.testing import cli_runner\nfrom persistence.testing import db_session\n\nheader = [\"account\", \"name\"]\nrows = [[\"ZQLA1\", \"Load One\"], [\"ZQLA2\", \"Load Two\"], [\"ZQLA3\", \"Load Two\"]]\nwith db_session() as db:\n    code, out, err = cli_runner(\"init\")\n    assert code == 0\n    with tempfile.TemporaryDirectory() as tmp:\n        path = os.path.join(tmp, \"accounts.csv\")\n        with open(path, \"w\", newline=\"\") as fh:\n            csv.writer(fh, lineterminator=\"\\n\").writerows([header] + rows)\n        code, out, err = cli_runner(\"load\", \"accounts\", path)\n    print(err)\n    assert code == 0\n    assert list(csv.reader(io.StringIO(out))) == [[\"table\", \"loaded\"], [\"accounts\", str(len(rows))]]\n    stored = [[r.account, r.name] for r in db.accounts.list()]\n    assert stored == sorted(rows)\n=== END AC load-accounts-success ===\n\n=== AC load-accounts-header-only ===\nIntent: A header-only file loads zero rows and reports zero.\n\nimport csv\nimport io\nimport os\nimport tempfile\nfrom
